{"forms":[{"form":"betweene","tags":["alternative","archaic"]},{"form":"betwene","tags":["alternative","archaic"]},{"form":"b/w","tags":["alternative","abbreviation"]},{"form":"btwn","tags":["alternative","abbreviation"]},{"form":"btw","tags":["alternative","abbreviation"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"prep","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"John stood between Amy and Mary.    Let’s meet between two and three.","type":"example"},{"text":"I want to buy one that costs somewhere between forty and fifty dollars.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["In the position or interval that separates (two things), or intermediate in quantity or degree. (See Usage notes below.)"]},{"examples":[{"text":"conversation between friends","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Done together or reciprocally."]},{"examples":[{"text":"Between you and me, I think the boss is crazy.  Let's keep this between ourselves.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Shared in confidence."]},{"examples":[{"text":"He's between jobs right now.  The shuttle runs between the town and the airport.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["In transit from (one to the other, or connecting places)."]},{"examples":[{"text":"Between us all, we shall succeed.  We've only got £5 between us.","type":"example"},{"text":"Between the leaky taps and the peeling wallpaper, there isn't much about this house to appeal to a buyer.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Combined (by effort or ownership)."]},{"examples":[{"text":"You must choose between him and me.","type":"example"},{"text":"Some colour-blind people can't distinguish between red and green.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["One of (representing a choice)."]},{"examples":[{"text":"Between the food and the card games, this proved to be the best birthday party I have ever had.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Taking together the combined effect of."]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/bɪˈtwiːn/","tags":["UK"]},{"ipa":"/bɪˈtwɪjn/","tags":["British","Southern","Standard"]},{"ipa":"/bəˈtwin/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/bɪˈtwin/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"[bɪˈtʰwin]","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/biˈtwin/","tags":["General-American"]}],"synonyms":[{"tags":["archaic"],"word":"atween"},{"tags":["dialectal"],"word":"atwix"},{"tags":["archaic"],"word":"atwixt"},{"tags":["archaic"],"word":"betwixt"},{"tags":["archaic"],"word":"betwixte"}],"upstream_line":36189,"upstream_offset":254368816,"upstream_record_sha256":"fa239630db8677b8eff501b99bde401715a02c6e0a7d306d66de8ca247288cd7","word":"between"}

{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"raves","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"raving","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"raved","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"raved","tags":["past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"glosses":["To be mentally unclear; to be delirious; to talk or act irrationally; to be wild, furious, or raging."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"glosses":["To speak or write wildly or incoherently."],"synonyms":[{"word":"rant"}],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"He raved about her beauty.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To talk or otherwise express oneself with excessive enthusiasm, passion or excitement."],"qualifier":"\"of\"; \"for\"; \"against\" or (formerly) \"on\"; \"of\"; \"for\"; \"against\" or (formerly) \"on\"","raw_tags":["followed by \"about\""],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"She raved that she must seek revenge against the wrongdoers.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To speak (a message) with intense enthusiasm, passion or excitement."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To rush wildly or furiously."],"tags":["obsolete"]},{"glosses":["To attend a rave (dance party)."],"tags":["intransitive"]}],"sounds":[{"enpr":"rāv"},{"ipa":"/ɹeɪv/"}],"synonyms":[{"word":"craze"},{"tags":["Australia"],"word":"have a kangaroo loose in the top paddock"},{"word":"have a screw loose"},{"word":"have bats in one's belfry"},{"word":"have bats in the belfry"},{"word":"have squirrels in the attic"},{"word":"have toys in the attic"},{"tags":["US","slang"],"word":"nut out"},{"tags":["slang"],"word":"nut up"},{"word":"rave"}],"upstream_line":238846,"upstream_offset":1179998637,"upstream_record_sha256":"4027387733487a242095eaeaabdfdd5e5bef6c0e83c315dfef015646cf1e48ff","word":"rave"}
